# Why It Sucks to Be Tall (in 1730s Prussia) — Image Prompts

333 beats · ~3–5 s each · Visual Style Profile locked · Characters locked to the Yesteryears character sheets

**Headwear rule:** headwear fits the character's class and the scene. Nobody wears a hat indoors or in any bad scenario (kidnapping, punishment, the gauntlet, the walk home). Outdoors as a poor miller's son, you wear a worn felt cap; as a boy, a tweed cap. The tall grenadier cap appears only on parade and in the uniform-fitting scenes, because the script calls for it. In the King's bedroom the giants carry their caps under their arms.

**Character lock**

| Story role | Sheet |
|---|---|
| You (18 and 28) | Mascot, drawn very tall |
| You, age 5 / 10 / 14 | Boy variant |
| Your mother | Adult woman variant |
| Old townswoman | Old woman variant |
| Father, Seamus, the King, sergeant, recruiter, clerk, officer, shepherd, student, blacksmith, Frederick, visitors | Not on a sheet (fixed descriptions) |
